        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><h1>v5.6.1 - Flash Attention Fix for XLM-R and RoBERTa Models</h1>
<p>This patch release fixes silently degraded embeddings for RoBERTa-family models when flash attention is requested with <code>transformers</code> v5, notably every XLM-R based multilingual embedding model (<code>BAAI/bge-m3</code>, <code>intfloat/multilingual-e5-large</code>, etc.). The bug affected v5.5.0, v5.5.1, and v5.6.0.</p>
<p>Install this version with</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Training + Inference
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.6.1

# Inference only, use one of:
pip install sentence-transformers==5.6.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.6.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.6.1
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.6.1

# Multimodal dependencies (optional):
pip install sentence-transformers[image]==5.6.1
pip install sentence-transformers[audio]==5.6.1
pip install sentence-transformers[video]==5.6.1

# Or combine as needed:
pip install sentence-transformers[train,onnx,image]==5.6.1"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Training + Inference</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.6.1

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Inference only, use one of:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers==5.6.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.6.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.6.1
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.6.1

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Multimodal dependencies (optional):</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[image]==5.6.1
pip install sentence-transformers[audio]==5.6.1
pip install sentence-transformers[video]==5.6.1

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or combine as needed:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train,onnx,image]==5.6.1</pre></div>
<h2>Fix position_ids offsetting for RoBERTa-family models when flattening inputs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4957028889" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3879" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3879/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3879">#3879</a>)</h2>
<p>Since v5.5.0, the <code>Transformer</code> module flattens text-only batches into one packed sequence when flash attention is requested, skipping all padding overhead for a notable performance improvement. The <code>position_ids</code> of that packed sequence restart at 0 for every text, which is correct for the vast majority of models. RoBERTa-family architectures however compute positions as <code>padding_idx + 1 + n</code> for the n-th token, so every token read a position embedding shifted by <code>padding_idx + 1</code> (usually 2). Nothing crashes, the embeddings are just silently worse.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

# An affected configuration: flash attention with an XLM-R based model
model = SentenceTransformer(
    &quot;BAAI/bge-m3&quot;,
    model_kwargs={&quot;attn_implementation&quot;: &quot;flash_attention_2&quot;},
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">SentenceTransformer</span>

<span class="pl-c"># An affected configuration: flash attention with an XLM-R based model</span>
<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformer</span>(
    <span class="pl-s">"BAAI/bge-m3"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">model_kwargs</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>{<span class="pl-s">"attn_implementation"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"flash_attention_2"</span>},
)</pre></div>
<p>Measured on <code>BAAI/bge-m3</code>:</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Evaluation</th>
<th>padded</th>
<th>packed, 0-based positions</th>
<th>packed, with this fix</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>stsb test Spearman</td>
<td>0.8485</td>
<td>0.7239</td>
<td>0.8485</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>NanoBEIR mean nDCG@10</td>
<td>0.6041</td>
<td>0.5414</td>
<td>0.6050</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>The quality loss recovers exactly once the offset is applied. The fix scans the loaded model's modules once for an int <code>padding_idx</code> stored next to a learned <code>position_embeddings</code> table, and offsets the packed <code>position_ids</code> when that pair is found. An audit of <code>transformers</code> finds 16 architectures with that pair (<code>roberta</code>, <code>xlm_roberta</code>, <code>xlm_roberta_xl</code>, <code>camembert</code>, <code>roberta_prelayernorm</code>, <code>xmod</code>, <code>data2vec_text</code>, <code>longformer</code>, <code>luke</code>, <code>ibert</code>, <code>mpnet</code>, <code>markuplm</code>, <code>lilt</code>, <code>layoutlmv3</code>, <code>esm</code>, and <code>pp_doclayout_v2</code>), all offset by exactly <code>padding_idx + 1</code>, and no 0-based or rotary architecture matches.</p>
<p>You are only affected if you encoded text with flash attention requested on <code>transformers</code> v5 with a RoBERTa-family checkpoint. The default padded path (e.g. <code>sdpa</code>) was never affected, and neither were MPNet models like <code>all-mpnet-base-v2</code> despite <code>mpnet</code> appearing in the audit: <code>transformers</code> does not support flash attention for MPNet at all. If you did index a corpus with such a configuration, re-encode it after upgrading: pre-fix embeddings score notably worse and do not mix with post-fix embeddings.</p>
<h2>What's Changed</h2>
<ul>
<li>[<code>ci</code>] Exclude librosa/numba/llvmlite on Python 3.13 by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/tomaarsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/tomaarsen">@tomaarsen</a> in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4751815173" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3835" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3835/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3835">#3835</a></li>
<li>[<code>tests</code>] Skip bf16 + Windows + CPU forwards, as they can WindowsError on torch 2.13 by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/tomaarsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/tomaarsen">@tomaarsen</a> in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4892601792" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3863" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3863/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3863">#3863</a></li>
<li>🚨 Fix position_ids offsetting for RoBERTa-family models when flattening inputs by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/tomaarsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/tomaarsen">@tomaarsen</a> in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4957028889" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3879" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3879/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3879">#3879</a></li>
</ul>

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><p>This minor version is a correctness- and robustness-focused release. It fixes a silent scoring bug for causal-LM rerankers, corrects several hard-negative mining and GIST loss edge cases, restores TSDAE on <code>transformers</code> v5, and adds Apple Silicon (MPS) support for the cached losses.</p>
<p>The headline fix affects chat-template models that read the final token position, i.e. causal-LM rerankers (like <code>Qwen3-Reranker</code>) and last-token-pooling embedders: when an over-long input was truncated, the chat template's trailing suffix (e.g. the assistant prefill the model scores from) was silently dropped, producing wrong scores with no error. There's also a forward-looking deprecation: loading local custom code without <code>trust_remote_code=True</code> now warns, and will require it from v6.0.</p>
<p>Install this version with</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Training + Inference
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.6.0

# Inference only, use one of:
pip install sentence-transformers==5.6.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.6.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.6.0
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.6.0

# Multimodal dependencies (optional):
pip install sentence-transformers[image]==5.6.0
pip install sentence-transformers[audio]==5.6.0
pip install sentence-transformers[video]==5.6.0

# Or combine as needed:
pip install sentence-transformers[train,onnx,image]==5.6.0"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Training + Inference</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.6.0

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Inference only, use one of:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers==5.6.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.6.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.6.0
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.6.0

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Multimodal dependencies (optional):</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[image]==5.6.0
pip install sentence-transformers[audio]==5.6.0
pip install sentence-transformers[video]==5.6.0

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or combine as needed:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train,onnx,image]==5.6.0</pre></div>
<h2>Fixed silently wrong scores when truncation drops chat-template suffixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4530579010" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3787" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3787/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3787">#3787</a>)</h2>
<p>Chat-template models render the full conversation to a flat string before tokenizing, so when the rendered input is longer than the tokenizer's <code>model_max_length</code>, the tokenizer truncates it from the right and drops the template's trailing suffix: the fixed tokens a template appends <em>after</em> the content, e.g. a prompt, instruction, <code>[/INST]</code>, or a trailing EOS. For models that read the final token position, this silently corrupted the result:</p>
<ul>
<li>causal-LM rerankers (e.g. <code>Qwen/Qwen3-Reranker-0.6B</code>) score a pair from the last token's <code>yes</code>/<code>no</code> logits, and</li>
<li>last-token-pooling embedders read the final hidden state.</li>
</ul>
<p>When the suffix was truncated away, that final position landed mid-document instead of after the prefill, so the score or embedding came from the wrong place.</p>
<p><code>Transformer.preprocess</code> now detects when truncation drops the suffix and splices it back onto the tail of each truncated row. Because the fix lives in the shared base <code>Transformer</code>, it applies across <code>SentenceTransformer</code>, <code>CrossEncoder</code>, and <code>SparseEncoder</code>. It's enabled by default and saved to the model configuration. Pass <code>processing_kwargs={"chat_template": {"restore_suffix": False}}</code> to opt back into raw truncation.</p>
<h2>Hard-negative mining and GIST loss correctness (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4655146142" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3821" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3821/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3821">#3821</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4646988708" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3817" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3817/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3817">#3817</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4645843445" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3816" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3816/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3816">#3816</a>)</h2>
<p>A trio of correctness and scalability fixes for hard-negative mining and the GIST losses:</p>
<ul>
<li>Sign-independent relative margin: <code>mine_hard_negatives(relative_margin=...)</code> and the <code>margin_strategy="relative"</code> branch of <code>GISTEmbedLoss</code> / <code>CachedGISTEmbedLoss</code> used a multiplicative threshold (<code>positive * (1 - margin)</code>) that only behaves correctly when the positive-pair similarity is positive. When that similarity was negative, the threshold moved the wrong way and let through false negatives: candidates <em>more</em> similar to the anchor than the true positive. The threshold is now <code>positive - |positive| * margin</code>, identical to before for positive scores but correct for negative ones.</li>
<li>Distributed positive masking in the GIST losses: with <code>gather_across_devices=True</code> and a non-zero <code>margin</code>, the false-negative suppression mask protected the wrong columns on ranks beyond the first (it ignored the per-rank offset into the gathered batch), which set the true positive's logit to <code>-inf</code> and produced a <code>+inf</code> loss. The mask now accounts for the cross-rank offset, so multi-GPU GIST training stays finite.</li>
<li>Memory-bounded mining without FAISS: <code>mine_hard_negatives(use_faiss=False)</code> (the default) materialized the full <code>(queries × corpus)</code> similarity matrix at once, which could OOM on large corpora. It now batches over the query axis (controlled by <code>faiss_batch_size</code>, default 16384), bounding peak memory while producing identical results.</li>
</ul>
<h2>TSDAE weight tying restored on <code>transformers</code> v5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4509933503" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3781" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3781/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3781">#3781</a>)</h2>
<p><code>transformers</code> v5 removed the private <code>PreTrainedModel._tie_encoder_decoder_weights</code> helper that <code>DenoisingAutoEncoderLoss</code> (TSDAE) used to tie its separate encoder and decoder. As a stopgap, v5.5 raised a <code>RuntimeError</code> for the default <code>tie_encoder_decoder=True</code> on <code>transformers &gt;= 5.0.0</code>, effectively breaking TSDAE there unless you pinned an older <code>transformers</code> or disabled tying. TSDAE now ships its own tying routine that shares storage between encoder and decoder, so it works on both <code>transformers</code> &lt;5 and &gt;=5 with the default settings.</p>
<h2>Deprecation: loading local custom code without <code>trust_remote_code</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4620748531" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3807" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3807/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3807">#3807</a>)</h2>
<p>Sentence Transformers has historically treated any local model directory as implicitly trusted: local custom code (e.g. <code>modeling_*.py</code>) loaded even with <code>trust_remote_code=False</code>, unlike <code>transformers</code>. This discrepancy might be unexpected, so loading local custom code this way now emits a <code>FutureWarning</code>, and from <strong>v6.0</strong> it will require <code>trust_remote_code=True</code> like in <code>transformers</code>.</p>
<h2>Apple Silicon (MPS) support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4632768044" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3812" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3812/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3812">#3812</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4647993455" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3818" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3818/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3818">#3818</a>)</h2>
<p>Two fixes for training on Apple Silicon:</p>
<ul>
<li>Cached losses on MPS: <code>CachedMultipleNegativesRankingLoss</code> and <code>CachedGISTEmbedLoss</code> crashed at construction on MPS because their <code>RandContext</code> used a CUDA-only RNG path. They now run on MPS with deterministic replay preserved.</li>
<li>Legacy fit path and <code>SparseEncoder</code> sparsity on MPS: the legacy <code>model.fit(..., use_amp=True)</code> path hard-coded CUDA's AMP <code>GradScaler</code> / <code>autocast</code>, and <code>SparseEncoder</code> sparsity statistics called <code>to_sparse_csr()</code>, which is unimplemented on MPS. Both now work on Apple Silicon.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Bug Fixes</h2>
<ul>
<li>Cast learning-to-rank loss logits to float32 in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4601999896" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3800" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3800/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3800">#3800</a>: the listwise learning-to-rank losses scatter the model's logits into a float32 matrix, which crashed with a dtype mismatch when the model itself was in bf16/fp16 and trained <em>without</em> <code>bf16=True</code>/<code>fp16=True</code> (with those enabled, autocast outputs float32 logits, so the common path was unaffected). Logits are now upcast to float32 in the loss.</li>
<li>Don't override <code>device_map</code> placement with the <code>device</code> argument in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4673461401" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3823" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3823/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3823">#3823</a>: loading with <code>model_kwargs={"device_map": ...}</code> previously placed the backbone via <code>accelerate</code>, then immediately moved it to the default device, defeating <code>device_map</code>. It now keeps the backbone in place (moving the other modules onto its device), and warns if both <code>device</code> and <code>device_map</code> are passed.</li>
<li>Collapse single-key multimodal dicts to the bare modality in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4479129144" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3779" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3779/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3779">#3779</a>: a single-key input like <code>{"image": img}</code> was classified as a combined modality and rejected by models that support only that one modality (e.g. BGE-VL), with a self-contradicting error. It is now treated as the bare <code>"image"</code> input, which unblocks vision-retrieval benchmarks like MTEB that pass <code>{"image": ...}</code>.</li>
<li>Clarify unsupported-modality error messages in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4572103675" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3792" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3792/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3792">#3792</a>: mixed or combined multimodal inputs on models that can't fuse modalities produced confusing errors (e.g. <code>Modality 'message' is not supported</code>). The errors are now scenario-specific and suggest what to do, such as encoding each modality separately.</li>
<li>Guard distributed APIs in <code>get_device_name</code> in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4594096232" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3798" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3798/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3798">#3798</a>: on PyTorch builds where <code>torch.distributed</code> is present but unavailable (some ROCm and CPU-only builds), <code>get_device_name()</code> crashed with <code>AttributeError: module 'torch.distributed' has no attribute 'is_initialized'</code>. It now checks <code>is_available()</code> first, across all distributed call sites.</li>
<li>Fix OpenVINO static quantization for optimum-intel 2.0 / OpenVINO 2026 in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4641936157" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3814" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3814/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3814">#3814</a>: <code>export_static_quantized_openvino_model</code> defaulted its calibration dataset to the bare id <code>"glue"</code>, which the stricter Hub repo-id validation now rejects. The default is now the namespaced <code>"nyu-mll/glue"</code>.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Examples, Documentation, and Notebooks</h2>
<p>A batch of example and documentation modernization, mostly migrating example scripts off deprecated <code>datasets</code> script-loaders and bare ids onto maintained Hugging Face datasets so they run on <code>datasets</code> 4.x:</p>
<ul>
<li>Fix example datasets that crash on <code>datasets</code> 4.x in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4510828930" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3782" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3782/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3782">#3782</a> (e.g. <code>quora</code>, <code>nq_open</code>, <code>yahoo_answers_topics</code>).</li>
<li>Migrate the MS MARCO examples to <code>datasets</code> in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4510893623" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3783" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3783/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3783">#3783</a>.</li>
<li>Migrate the multilingual parallel-sentences data-prep scripts to <code>datasets</code> in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4511674962" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3784" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3784/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3784">#3784</a>.</li>
<li>Migrate the Quora semantic-search and clustering examples to <code>datasets</code> in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4511852734" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3785" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3785/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3785">#3785</a>.</li>
<li>Modernize the AugSBERT data-augmentation STSb scripts in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4618761375" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3806" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3806/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3806">#3806</a>, also fixing the QQP cross-domain script to use the <code>quora-duplicates</code> labels it previously ignored.</li>
<li>Refresh the CLIP / image-search notebooks in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4485121766" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3780" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3780/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3780">#3780</a>, and modernize the CLIP training notebook in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4618739077" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3805" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3805/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3805">#3805</a>.</li>
<li>Fix a reStructuredText table in the <code>ContrastiveTensionLoss</code> docstring in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4555903925" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3788" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3788/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3788">#3788</a>, and add a low-VRAM hardware note to the efficiency docs in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4612130855" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3802" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3802/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3802">#3802</a>.</li>
<li>Fix package build warnings and errors in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4622377733" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3809" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3809/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3809">#3809</a>, and a batch of Sphinx doc-build problems in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4631680602" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3811" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3811/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3811">#3811</a>.</li>

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><p>This patch release fixes a small quirk with multimodal inference when using single-key multimodal inputs like <code>model.encode({"image": ...})</code>.</p>
<p>Install this version with</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Training + Inference
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.5.1

# Inference only, use one of:
pip install sentence-transformers==5.5.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.5.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.5.1
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.5.1

# Multimodal dependencies (optional):
pip install sentence-transformers[image]==5.5.1
pip install sentence-transformers[audio]==5.5.1
pip install sentence-transformers[video]==5.5.1

# Or combine as needed:
pip install sentence-transformers[train,onnx,image]==5.5.1"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Training + Inference</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.5.1

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Inference only, use one of:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers==5.5.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.5.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.5.1
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.5.1

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Multimodal dependencies (optional):</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[image]==5.5.1
pip install sentence-transformers[audio]==5.5.1
pip install sentence-transformers[video]==5.5.1

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or combine as needed:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train,onnx,image]==5.5.1</pre></div>
<h2>Bug fixed</h2>
<p>Previously, inference like <code>model.encode({"image": ...})</code> or <code>model.encode([{"image": ...}, ...])</code> would be inferred as the <code>("image",)</code> modality, which differed from the inferred modality of <code>"image"</code> for just <code>model.encode(my_image)</code> or <code>model.encode([my_image, my_image_2, ...])</code>.</p>
<p>This results in confusing errors if the model doesn't have a <code>modality_config</code> mapping for <code>("image",)</code> in addition to <code>"image"</code>, so now a single-key multimodal dict is collapsed to the bare modality (just <code>"image"</code> in this example).</p>
<p>This affected this code:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

model = SentenceTransformer('BAAI/BGE-VL-base', trust_remote_code=True)
embedding = model.encode({&quot;image&quot;: &quot;https://huggingface.co/datasets/huggingface/documentation-images/resolve/main/blog/ettin-reranker/mteb_ndcg10_all-MiniLM-L6-v2.png&quot;})
print(embedding.shape)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">SentenceTransformer</span>

<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformer</span>(<span class="pl-s">'BAAI/BGE-VL-base'</span>, <span class="pl-s1">trust_remote_code</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">embedding</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">encode</span>({<span class="pl-s">"image"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"https://huggingface.co/datasets/huggingface/documentation-images/resolve/main/blog/ettin-reranker/mteb_ndcg10_all-MiniLM-L6-v2.png"</span>})
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">embedding</span>.<span class="pl-c1">shape</span>)</pre></div>
<p>Which previously failed as the model only implements a path for <code>"text"</code>, <code>"image"</code>, and <code>("image", "text")</code>.</p>
<h2>All Changes</h2>
<ul>
<li>[fix] Collapse single-key multimodal dicts to bare modality by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/tomaarsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/tomaarsen">@tomaarsen</a> in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4479129144" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3779" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3779/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3779">#3779</a></li>
</ul>

        <h2 class="sr-only" id="hd-961f7d9d">v5.5.0 - Training Agent Skill, EmbedDistillLoss, and ADRMSELoss</h2>

        <div class="tmp-my-5">
          <div class=""   >
  <div data-view-component="true" class="Box">
  
  <div data-view-component="true" class="Box-body">      <div class="d-flex flex-md-row flex-column">

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><p>This release ships the <code>train-sentence-transformers</code> Agent Skill, adds two new training losses, and brings a long list of robustness and correctness fixes.</p>
<p>The new <code>train-sentence-transformers</code> Agent Skill lets AI coding agents (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Gemini CLI, ...) drive end-to-end training and fine-tuning across all three model types. <code>EmbedDistillLoss</code> is a new embedding-level knowledge distillation loss for <code>SentenceTransformer</code>: it aligns a student model's embeddings with pre-computed teacher embeddings, an alternative to the score-based distillation provided by <code>MarginMSELoss</code> and <code>DistillKLDivLoss</code>. <code>ADRMSELoss</code> is a new listwise learning-to-rank loss for <code>CrossEncoder</code> from the Rank-DistiLLM paper. <code>encode()</code> and <code>predict()</code> also gain a per-call <code>processing_kwargs</code> override, and more.</p>
<p>Install this version with</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Training + Inference
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.5.0

# Inference only, use one of:
pip install sentence-transformers==5.5.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.5.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.5.0
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.5.0

# Multimodal dependencies (optional):
pip install sentence-transformers[image]==5.5.0
pip install sentence-transformers[audio]==5.5.0
pip install sentence-transformers[video]==5.5.0

# Or combine as needed:
pip install sentence-transformers[train,onnx,image]==5.5.0"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Training + Inference</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.5.0

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Inference only, use one of:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers==5.5.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.5.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.5.0
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.5.0

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Multimodal dependencies (optional):</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[image]==5.5.0
pip install sentence-transformers[audio]==5.5.0
pip install sentence-transformers[video]==5.5.0

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or combine as needed:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train,onnx,image]==5.5.0</pre></div>
<h2>The <code>train-sentence-transformers</code> Agent Skill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4390984208" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3752" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3752/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3752">#3752</a>)</h2>
<p>If you use an AI coding agent (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Gemini CLI, OpenCode, ...), you can now install the <a href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/tree/main/skills"><code>train-sentence-transformers</code></a> Agent Skill and ask your agent to fine-tune a model on your data:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="hf skills add train-sentence-transformers              # installs under ./.agents/skills/
hf skills add train-sentence-transformers --global     # installs under ~/.agents/skills/
hf skills add train-sentence-transformers --claude     # also symlinks into .claude/skills/"><pre>hf skills add train-sentence-transformers              <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> installs under ./.agents/skills/</span>
hf skills add train-sentence-transformers --global     <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> installs under ~/.agents/skills/</span>
hf skills add train-sentence-transformers --claude     <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> also symlinks into .claude/skills/</span></pre></div>
<p>The skill gives the agent curated, version-aware guidance for training <code>SentenceTransformer</code> (bi-encoder), <code>CrossEncoder</code> (reranker), and <code>SparseEncoder</code>/SPLADE models, covering base model selection, loss and evaluator choice, hard-negative mining, distillation, LoRA, Matryoshka, multilingual training, static embeddings, plus a set of production-ready training template scripts. Then you can prompt your agent with things like:</p>
<blockquote>
<p>"Train a multilingual sentence-transformer on Dutch legal pairs."</p>
<p>"Fine-tune a cross-encoder reranker on <code>(question, answer)</code> pairs from my dataset, mine hard negatives, and push to my Hub repo."</p>
<p>"Train a German sparse embedding model with high sparsity."</p>
<p>"Can you train a static embedding model on 100k code triplets?"</p>
</blockquote>
<p>The skill lives in the repository under <a href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/tree/main/skills"><code>skills/train-sentence-transformers/</code></a> and is mirrored to the <a href="https://github.com/huggingface/skills"><code>huggingface/skills</code></a> marketplace on each release.</p>
<h2>New loss: EmbedDistillLoss (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3958601033" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3665" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3665/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3665">#3665</a>)</h2>
<p>Introduces <code>EmbedDistillLoss</code> (<a href="https://huggingface.co/papers/2301.12005" rel="nofollow">Kim et al., 2023</a>), an embedding-level knowledge distillation loss for <code>SentenceTransformer</code>. Rather than distilling teacher <em>scores</em> (<code>MarginMSELoss</code>, <code>DistillKLDivLoss</code>), it directly aligns the student's <code>sentence_embedding</code> with a <strong>pre-computed teacher embedding</strong> passed via the dataset's <code>label</code> column. The comparison uses a configurable <code>distance_metric</code>, one of <code>"cosine"</code> (the default), <code>"l2"</code>, or <code>"mse"</code>. When the student and teacher dimensions differ, pass <code>projection_dim=&lt;teacher_dim&gt;</code> to add a learnable projection from the student's embedding space into the teacher's. That projection lives on the loss rather than on the saved model, so use <code>loss.save_projection(...)</code> / <code>loss.load_projection(...)</code> to reuse it across stages (e.g. like done in <a href="https://huggingface.co/papers/2602.15547" rel="nofollow">Arkam et al. for Jina v5</a>). As part of this change, <code>MSELoss</code> is now a thin subclass of <code>EmbedDistillLoss</code> with <code>distance_metric="mse"</code>, and also gains the optional <code>projection_dim</code> argument.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from datasets import Dataset
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer, SentenceTransformerTrainer
from sentence_transformers.sentence_transformer.losses import EmbedDistillLoss

student_model = SentenceTransformer(&quot;microsoft/mpnet-base&quot;)
teacher_model = SentenceTransformer(&quot;all-mpnet-base-v2&quot;)

train_dataset = Dataset.from_dict({
    &quot;sentence&quot;: [&quot;It's nice weather outside today.&quot;, &quot;He drove to work.&quot;],
})

# Pre-compute teacher embeddings once and store them as the `label` column
def add_teacher_embeddings(batch):
    return {&quot;label&quot;: teacher_model.encode(batch[&quot;sentence&quot;]).tolist()}

train_dataset = train_dataset.map(add_teacher_embeddings, batched=True)

loss = EmbedDistillLoss(student_model, distance_metric=&quot;cosine&quot;)
# If the student and teacher dimensions differ, add a learnable projection:
# loss = EmbedDistillLoss(student_model, distance_metric=&quot;cosine&quot;, projection_dim=768)

trainer = SentenceTransformerTrainer(
    model=student_model,
    train_dataset=train_dataset,
    loss=loss,
)
trainer.train()"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">datasets</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Dataset</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">SentenceTransformer</span>, <span class="pl-v">SentenceTransformerTrainer</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span>.<span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformer</span>.<span class="pl-s1">losses</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">EmbedDistillLoss</span>

<span class="pl-s1">student_model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformer</span>(<span class="pl-s">"microsoft/mpnet-base"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">teacher_model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformer</span>(<span class="pl-s">"all-mpnet-base-v2"</span>)

<span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">Dataset</span>.<span class="pl-c1">from_dict</span>({
    <span class="pl-s">"sentence"</span>: [<span class="pl-s">"It's nice weather outside today."</span>, <span class="pl-s">"He drove to work."</span>],
})

<span class="pl-c"># Pre-compute teacher embeddings once and store them as the `label` column</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">add_teacher_embeddings</span>(<span class="pl-s1">batch</span>):
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> {<span class="pl-s">"label"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">teacher_model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">encode</span>(<span class="pl-s1">batch</span>[<span class="pl-s">"sentence"</span>]).<span class="pl-c1">tolist</span>()}

<span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span>.<span class="pl-c1">map</span>(<span class="pl-s1">add_teacher_embeddings</span>, <span class="pl-s1">batched</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>)

<span class="pl-s1">loss</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">EmbedDistillLoss</span>(<span class="pl-s1">student_model</span>, <span class="pl-s1">distance_metric</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"cosine"</span>)
<span class="pl-c"># If the student and teacher dimensions differ, add a learnable projection:</span>
<span class="pl-c"># loss = EmbedDistillLoss(student_model, distance_metric="cosine", projection_dim=768)</span>

<span class="pl-s1">trainer</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformerTrainer</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">student_model</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">loss</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">loss</span>,
)
<span class="pl-s1">trainer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">train</span>()</pre></div>
<p>See the updated <a href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/tree/main/examples/sentence_transformer/training/distillation">model distillation examples</a> and the <a href="https://sbert.net/docs/sentence_transformer/loss_overview.html" rel="nofollow">loss overview</a> for more.</p>
<h2>New loss: ADRMSELoss for Cross Encoders (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4078191352" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3690" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3690/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3690">#3690</a>)</h2>
<p>Introduces <code>ADRMSELoss</code> (Approx Discounted Rank Mean Squared Error), a listwise learning-to-rank loss for <code>CrossEncoder</code> from the Rank-DistiLLM paper (<a href="https://huggingface.co/papers/2405.07920" rel="nofollow">Schlatt et al., ECIR 2025</a>). It computes a differentiable approximation of each document's rank via pairwise sigmoids and minimizes the nDCG-discounted squared error against the true ranks derived from the labels. It expects listwise inputs: a <code>(query, [doc1, ..., docN])</code> pair plus a <code>[score1, ..., scoreN]</code> label list per sample (binary or continuous labels, variable document counts allowed). It's designed for LLM-distillation reranking, where the per-document scores come from a strong LLM's ordering.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from datasets import Dataset
from sentence_transformers import CrossEncoder, CrossEncoderTrainer
from sentence_transformers.cross_encoder.losses import ADRMSELoss

model = CrossEncoder(&quot;microsoft/mpnet-base&quot;)
train_dataset = Dataset.from_dict({
    &quot;query&quot;: [&quot;What are pandas?&quot;, &quot;What is the capital of France?&quot;],
    &quot;docs&quot;: [
        [&quot;Pandas are a kind of bear.&quot;, &quot;Pandas are kind of like fish.&quot;],
        [&quot;The capital of France is Paris.&quot;, &quot;Paris is the capital of France.&quot;, &quot;Paris is quite large.&quot;],
    ],
    &quot;scores&quot;: [[0.95, 0.1], [0.98, 0.92, 0.2]],
})
loss = ADRMSELoss(model)

trainer = CrossEncoderTrainer(
    model=model,
    train_dataset=train_dataset,
    loss=loss,
)
trainer.train()"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">datasets</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Dataset</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">CrossEncoder</span>, <span class="pl-v">CrossEncoderTrainer</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span>.<span class="pl-s1">cross_encoder</span>.<span class="pl-s1">losses</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">ADRMSELoss</span>

<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">CrossEncoder</span>(<span class="pl-s">"microsoft/mpnet-base"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">Dataset</span>.<span class="pl-c1">from_dict</span>({
    <span class="pl-s">"query"</span>: [<span class="pl-s">"What are pandas?"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"What is the capital of France?"</span>],
    <span class="pl-s">"docs"</span>: [
        [<span class="pl-s">"Pandas are a kind of bear."</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Pandas are kind of like fish."</span>],
        [<span class="pl-s">"The capital of France is Paris."</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Paris is the capital of France."</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Paris is quite large."</span>],
    ],
    <span class="pl-s">"scores"</span>: [[<span class="pl-c1">0.95</span>, <span class="pl-c1">0.1</span>], [<span class="pl-c1">0.98</span>, <span class="pl-c1">0.92</span>, <span class="pl-c1">0.2</span>]],
})
<span class="pl-s1">loss</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">ADRMSELoss</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span>)

<span class="pl-s1">trainer</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">CrossEncoderTrainer</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">model</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">loss</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">loss</span>,
)
<span class="pl-s1">trainer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">train</span>()</pre></div>
<p>There's a full MS MARCO example at <a href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/blob/main/examples/cross_encoder/training/ms_marco/training_ms_marco_adrmse.py"><code>training_ms_marco_adrmse.py</code></a>. Note that <code>LambdaLoss</code> generally remains the strongest loss in the listwise family. See the <a href="https://sbert.net/docs/cross_encoder/loss_overview.html" rel="nofollow">Cross Encoder loss overview</a> for guidance on picking a loss.</p>
<h2>Per-call <code>processing_kwargs</code> override (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4391295260" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3753" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3753/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3753">#3753</a>)</h2>
<p><code>SentenceTransformer.encode()</code> / <code>encode_query()</code> / <code>encode_document()</code>, <code>SparseEncoder.encode()</code>, <code>CrossEncoder.predict()</code>, and <code>model.preprocess()</code> now accept a <code>processing_kwargs</code> argument that overrides the processor/tokenizer kwargs configured at construction time, for a single call. It has the same nested structure as the <code>processing_kwargs</code> constructor argument (top-level keys <code>text</code>, <code>audio</code>, <code>image</code>, <code>video</code>, <code>common</code>, <code>chat_template</code>) and is shallow-merged on top of the instance-level settings, so you can override just one setting (e.g. <code>max_length</code>) and leave the rest intact.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

model = SentenceTransformer(&quot;all-MiniLM-L6-v2&quot;)

# Override processor kwargs (e.g. max_length, truncation) for this call only:
embeddings = model.encode(
    [&quot;a short text&quot;, &quot;a much longer text that you want truncated more aggressively ...&quot;],
    processing_kwargs={&quot;text&quot;: {&quot;max_length&quot;: 256, &quot;truncation&quot;: True}},
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">SentenceTransformer</span>

<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformer</span>(<span class="pl-s">"all-MiniLM-L6-v2"</span>)

<span class="pl-c"># Override processor kwargs (e.g. max_length, truncation) for this call only:</span>
<span class="pl-s1">embeddings</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">encode</span>(
    [<span class="pl-s">"a short text"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"a much longer text that you want truncated more aggressively ..."</span>],
    <span class="pl-s1">processing_kwargs</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>{<span class="pl-s">"text"</span>: {<span class="pl-s">"max_length"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">256</span>, <span class="pl-s">"truncation"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">True</span>}},
)</pre></div>
<p>This is especially handy for vision-language models, where you can change the image resolution per call, e.g. <code>model.encode(images, processing_kwargs={"image": {"max_pixels": 256 * 256}})</code>.</p>
<h2>Smaller Features</h2>
<ul>
<li>Allow <code>CrossEncoder</code> module stacks that don't start with a <code>Transformer</code>, and recognize a trailing <code>Dense(module_output_name="scores")</code> as the scoring head, by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/tomaarsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/tomaarsen">@tomaarsen</a> in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4315542473" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3742" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3742/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3742">#3742</a>: <code>num_labels</code> now reads that head's <code>out_features</code>, and <code>model.config</code> / <code>model.model</code> return <code>None</code> when there's no underlying transformers model.</li>
<li>Infer that a model is an IR model on its generated model card when an <code>InformationRetrievalEvaluator</code> / <code>NanoBEIREvaluator</code> (or their sparse variants) was used during training, by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/tomaarsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/tomaarsen">@tomaarsen</a> in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4315223702" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3741" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3741/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3741">#3741</a>: the usage snippet then shows <code>encode_query</code> / <code>encode_document</code>, even without IR prompt names or a <code>Router</code> architecture.</li>
<li>Warn at model-load time when the installed <code>transformers</code> version is too old to honor <code>use_bidirectional_attention</code> / <code>is_causal</code> flags in a model's config (e.g. for <code>google/embeddinggemma-300m</code>), rather than silently ignoring them, by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/tomaarsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/tomaarsen">@tomaarsen</a> in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4267056373" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3726" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3726/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3726">#3726</a>.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Bug Fixes</h2>
<ul>
<li>Use the first non-pad token for CLS pooling with left-padding tokenizers by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/tomaarsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/tomaarsen">@tomaarsen</a> in <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4421619693" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3767" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3767/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3767">#3767</a>: <code>pooling_mode="cls"</code> previously returned the embedding at position 0, which is a <code>[PAD]</code> token for left-padded inputs (common with decoder-only models), silently producing incorrect sentence embeddings. It now ...</li>
</ul></div>
          <a href="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/releases/tag/v5.5.0" data-view-component="true" class="Link">Read more</a>

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><p>This patch release allows <code>encode()</code> and <code>predict()</code> to accept 1D numpy string arrays as inputs.</p>
<p>Install this version with</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Training + Inference
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.4.1

# Inference only, use one of:
pip install sentence-transformers==5.4.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.4.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.4.1
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.4.1

# Multimodal dependencies (optional):
pip install sentence-transformers[image]==5.4.1
pip install sentence-transformers[audio]==5.4.1
pip install sentence-transformers[video]==5.4.1

# Or combine as needed:
pip install sentence-transformers[train,onnx,image]==5.4.1"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Training + Inference</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.4.1

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Inference only, use one of:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers==5.4.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.4.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.4.1
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.4.1

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Multimodal dependencies (optional):</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[image]==5.4.1
pip install sentence-transformers[audio]==5.4.1
pip install sentence-transformers[video]==5.4.1

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or combine as needed:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train,onnx,image]==5.4.1</pre></div>
<h2>Numpy string/object arrays as batches (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4252506979" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3720" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3720/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3720">#3720</a>)</h2>
<p><code>encode()</code> and <code>predict()</code> now correctly recognize 1D numpy string/object arrays as batches rather than singular inputs. Previously, something like <code>model.encode(df["text"].to_numpy())</code> was silently treated as a single input and produced incorrect output. 1D numpy arrays with <code>dtype.kind</code> in <code>("U", "O")</code> are now unpacked like lists, and 2D+ arrays are treated as batches of pairs (for <code>CrossEncoder</code>).</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import numpy as np
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

model = SentenceTransformer(&quot;all-MiniLM-L6-v2&quot;)

# Previously treated as one input; now correctly encoded as 3 separate texts
embeddings = model.encode(np.array([&quot;first&quot;, &quot;second&quot;, &quot;third&quot;]))
print(embeddings.shape)
# (3, 384)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">numpy</span> <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-s1">np</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">SentenceTransformer</span>

<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformer</span>(<span class="pl-s">"all-MiniLM-L6-v2"</span>)

<span class="pl-c"># Previously treated as one input; now correctly encoded as 3 separate texts</span>
<span class="pl-s1">embeddings</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">encode</span>(<span class="pl-s1">np</span>.<span class="pl-c1">array</span>([<span class="pl-s">"first"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"second"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"third"</span>]))
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">embeddings</span>.<span class="pl-c1">shape</span>)
<span class="pl-c"># (3, 384)</span></pre></div>
<p>For <code>CrossEncoder</code>, a 1D numpy string array is still treated as a single <code>[query, document]</code> pair to match the existing list behavior, while a 2D array of shape <code>(N, 2)</code> is a batch of <code>N</code> pairs.</p>
<h2>Safer activation function loading in <code>Dense</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4238296978" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3714" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3714/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3714">#3714</a>)</h2>
<p>The <code>Dense</code> module stores its activation function as a dotted import path in its saved config (e.g. <code>"torch.nn.modules.activation.Tanh"</code>), which was then resolved via <code>import_from_string</code> whenever the module was loaded. Because any importable Python callable could be referenced, a maliciously crafted <code>config.json</code> on the Hub could trigger arbitrary imports at model load time.</p>
<p>The loader now only resolves activation functions whose import path starts with <code>torch.</code>. Anything else is skipped with a warning and replaced by the default activation (<code>Tanh</code>). To load a model with a custom (non-torch) activation function, opt in explicitly with <code>trust_remote_code=True</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

# Torch-provided activations load as before
model = SentenceTransformer(&quot;some/model-with-torch-activation&quot;)

# Non-torch activations now require explicit opt-in
model = SentenceTransformer(&quot;some/model-with-custom-activation&quot;, trust_remote_code=True)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">SentenceTransformer</span>

<span class="pl-c"># Torch-provided activations load as before</span>
<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformer</span>(<span class="pl-s">"some/model-with-torch-activation"</span>)

<span class="pl-c"># Non-torch activations now require explicit opt-in</span>
<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformer</span>(<span class="pl-s">"some/model-with-custom-activation"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">trust_remote_code</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>)</pre></div>
<p>This mirrors the opt-in trust model already used by <code>transformers</code> for custom code, and ensures untrusted model repositories cannot smuggle arbitrary imports through the <code>Dense</code> activation config.</p>
