        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><p>This large release introduces first-class multimodal support for both <code>SentenceTransformer</code> and <code>CrossEncoder</code>, making it easy to compute embeddings and rerank across text, images, audio, and video. The <code>CrossEncoder</code> class has been fully modularized, allowing for generative rerankers (CausalLM-based models) via a new <code>LogitScore</code> module. Flash Attention 2 now automatically skips padding for text-only inputs, providing significant speedups &amp; memory reductions, especially when input lengths vary.</p>
<blockquote>
<p><strong>Blog post</strong>: <a href="https://huggingface.co/blog/multimodal-sentence-transformers" rel="nofollow">Multimodal Embedding &amp; Reranker Models with Sentence Transformers</a>: a walkthrough of the new multimodal capabilities with some practical examples.</p>
<p><strong>Migration guide</strong>: <a href="https://sbert.net/docs/migration_guide.html" rel="nofollow">Migrating from v5.x to v5.4+</a>: covers updated import paths, renamed parameters, and other softly breaking changes with deprecation warnings. Note that there are no hard deprecations, all existing code should continue to work with warnings at worst.</p>
</blockquote>
<p>Install this version with</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Training + Inference
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.4.0

# Inference only, use one of:
pip install sentence-transformers==5.4.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.4.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.4.0
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.4.0

# Multimodal dependencies (optional):
pip install sentence-transformers[image]==5.4.0
pip install sentence-transformers[audio]==5.4.0
pip install sentence-transformers[video]==5.4.0

# Or combine as needed:
pip install sentence-transformers[train,onnx,image]==5.4.0"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Training + Inference</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.4.0

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Inference only, use one of:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers==5.4.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.4.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.4.0
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.4.0

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Multimodal dependencies (optional):</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[image]==5.4.0
pip install sentence-transformers[audio]==5.4.0
pip install sentence-transformers[video]==5.4.0

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or combine as needed:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train,onnx,image]==5.4.0</pre></div>
<h2>Multimodal Embeddings with SentenceTransformer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3549975221" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3554" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3554/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3554">#3554</a>)</h2>
<p><a href="https://sbert.net/docs/package_reference/sentence_transformer/SentenceTransformer.html#sentence_transformers.SentenceTransformer" rel="nofollow"><code>SentenceTransformer</code></a> now natively supports vision-language models (VLMs) and other multimodal architectures. You can encode and compare across text, images, audio, videos, or combinations of these, with automatic modality detection and preprocessing. Models advertise which modalities they support via the new <a href="https://sbert.net/docs/package_reference/sentence_transformer/SentenceTransformer.html#sentence_transformers.SentenceTransformer.modalities" rel="nofollow"><code>model.modalities</code></a> property and <a href="https://sbert.net/docs/package_reference/sentence_transformer/SentenceTransformer.html#sentence_transformers.SentenceTransformer.supports" rel="nofollow"><code>model.supports()</code></a> method.</p>
<h3>Using a pretrained multimodal embedding model</h3>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from PIL import Image
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

model = SentenceTransformer(
    &quot;Qwen/Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B&quot;,
    model_kwargs={&quot;attn_implementation&quot;: &quot;flash_attention_2&quot;, &quot;torch_dtype&quot;: &quot;bfloat16&quot;},
    processor_kwargs={&quot;min_pixels&quot;: 28 * 28, &quot;max_pixels&quot;: 600 * 600},
    revision=&quot;refs/pr/23&quot;,
)

# Check supported modalities
print(model.modalities)
# ['text', 'image', 'video', 'message']
print(model.supports(&quot;image&quot;))
# True

# Encode text
text_embeddings = model.encode([&quot;A photo of a cat&quot;, &quot;A pollinator on a flower&quot;])

# Encode images (PIL images, file paths, or URLs all work)
image_embeddings = model.encode([
    Image.open(&quot;cat.jpg&quot;),
    &quot;https://example.com/flower.jpg&quot;,
])

# Encode mixed text+image inputs
multimodal_embeddings = model.encode([
    {&quot;text&quot;: &quot;Describe this image&quot;, &quot;image&quot;: Image.open(&quot;cat.jpg&quot;)},
])

# Compute cross-modal similarity
similarity = model.similarity(text_embeddings, image_embeddings)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-c1">PIL</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Image</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">SentenceTransformer</span>

<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformer</span>(
    <span class="pl-s">"Qwen/Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">model_kwargs</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>{<span class="pl-s">"attn_implementation"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"flash_attention_2"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"torch_dtype"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"bfloat16"</span>},
    <span class="pl-s1">processor_kwargs</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>{<span class="pl-s">"min_pixels"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">28</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-c1">28</span>, <span class="pl-s">"max_pixels"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">600</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-c1">600</span>},
    <span class="pl-s1">revision</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"refs/pr/23"</span>,
)

<span class="pl-c"># Check supported modalities</span>
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">modalities</span>)
<span class="pl-c"># ['text', 'image', 'video', 'message']</span>
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">supports</span>(<span class="pl-s">"image"</span>))
<span class="pl-c"># True</span>

<span class="pl-c"># Encode text</span>
<span class="pl-s1">text_embeddings</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">encode</span>([<span class="pl-s">"A photo of a cat"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"A pollinator on a flower"</span>])

<span class="pl-c"># Encode images (PIL images, file paths, or URLs all work)</span>
<span class="pl-s1">image_embeddings</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">encode</span>([
    <span class="pl-v">Image</span>.<span class="pl-c1">open</span>(<span class="pl-s">"cat.jpg"</span>),
    <span class="pl-s">"https://example.com/flower.jpg"</span>,
])

<span class="pl-c"># Encode mixed text+image inputs</span>
<span class="pl-s1">multimodal_embeddings</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">encode</span>([
    {<span class="pl-s">"text"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Describe this image"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"image"</span>: <span class="pl-v">Image</span>.<span class="pl-c1">open</span>(<span class="pl-s">"cat.jpg"</span>)},
])

<span class="pl-c"># Compute cross-modal similarity</span>
<span class="pl-s1">similarity</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">similarity</span>(<span class="pl-s1">text_embeddings</span>, <span class="pl-s1">image_embeddings</span>)</pre></div>
<h3>Building multimodal models with Router</h3>
<p>You can also compose separate encoders for different modalities using the new <a href="https://sbert.net/docs/package_reference/base/modules.html#sentence_transformers.base.modules.Router" rel="nofollow"><code>Router</code></a> module. Unlike the single-backbone VLM approach, Router lets you combine any existing text and image encoders and route inputs based on detected modality:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
from sentence_transformers.sentence_transformer.modules import Dense, Pooling, Router, Transformer

# Text encoder: MiniLM with mean pooling, projected to 768 dims to match image encoder
text_encoder = Transformer(&quot;sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2&quot;)
text_pooling = Pooling(text_encoder.get_embedding_dimension(), pooling_mode=&quot;mean&quot;)
text_projection = Dense(text_encoder.get_embedding_dimension(), 768)

# Image encoder: SigLIP outputs pooled embeddings directly
image_encoder = Transformer(&quot;google/siglip2-base-patch16-224&quot;)

# Route inputs to the appropriate encoder based on detected modality
router = Router(
    sub_modules={
        &quot;text&quot;: [text_encoder, text_pooling, text_projection],
        &quot;image&quot;: [image_encoder],
    },
)

model = SentenceTransformer(modules=[router])

# Text and image inputs are automatically routed to the correct encoder
text_embeddings = model.encode([&quot;A photo of a cat&quot;])
image_embeddings = model.encode([&quot;https://example.com/cat.jpg&quot;])
similarity = model.similarity(text_embeddings, image_embeddings)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">SentenceTransformer</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span>.<span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformer</span>.<span class="pl-s1">modules</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Dense</span>, <span class="pl-v">Pooling</span>, <span class="pl-v">Router</span>, <span class="pl-v">Transformer</span>

<span class="pl-c"># Text encoder: MiniLM with mean pooling, projected to 768 dims to match image encoder</span>
<span class="pl-s1">text_encoder</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Transformer</span>(<span class="pl-s">"sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">text_pooling</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Pooling</span>(<span class="pl-s1">text_encoder</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get_embedding_dimension</span>(), <span class="pl-s1">pooling_mode</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"mean"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">text_projection</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Dense</span>(<span class="pl-s1">text_encoder</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get_embedding_dimension</span>(), <span class="pl-c1">768</span>)

<span class="pl-c"># Image encoder: SigLIP outputs pooled embeddings directly</span>
<span class="pl-s1">image_encoder</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Transformer</span>(<span class="pl-s">"google/siglip2-base-patch16-224"</span>)

<span class="pl-c"># Route inputs to the appropriate encoder based on detected modality</span>
<span class="pl-s1">router</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Router</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">sub_modules</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>{
        <span class="pl-s">"text"</span>: [<span class="pl-s1">text_encoder</span>, <span class="pl-s1">text_pooling</span>, <span class="pl-s1">text_projection</span>],
        <span class="pl-s">"image"</span>: [<span class="pl-s1">image_encoder</span>],
    },
)

<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformer</span>(<span class="pl-s1">modules</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[<span class="pl-s1">router</span>])

<span class="pl-c"># Text and image inputs are automatically routed to the correct encoder</span>
<span class="pl-s1">text_embeddings</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">encode</span>([<span class="pl-s">"A photo of a cat"</span>])
<span class="pl-s1">image_embeddings</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">encode</span>([<span class="pl-s">"https://example.com/cat.jpg"</span>])
<span class="pl-s1">similarity</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">similarity</span>(<span class="pl-s1">text_embeddings</span>, <span class="pl-s1">image_embeddings</span>)</pre></div>
<h2>Multimodal Reranking with CrossEncoder (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3549975221" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3554" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3554/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3554">#3554</a>)</h2>
<p><code>CrossEncoder</code> now supports multimodal inputs for reranking, enabling cross-modal scoring of query-document pairs where either side can be text, images, audio, video, or mixed-modality content. This works with both generative rerankers (CausalLM-based, via the new <a href="https://sbert.net/docs/package_reference/cross_encoder/modules.html#sentence_transformers.cross_encoder.modules.LogitScore" rel="nofollow"><code>LogitScore</code></a> module) and encoder-based models. See the <a href="https://sbert.net/docs/cross_encoder/pretrained_models.html#multimodal-rerankers" rel="nofollow">pretrained multimodal rerankers</a> for models you can use right away.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from sentence_transformers import CrossEncoder

# Load a multimodal reranker
model = CrossEncoder(&quot;Qwen/Qwen3-VL-Reranker-2B&quot;, revision=&quot;refs/pr/11&quot;)

# Rank text documents against an image query (or vice versa)
results = model.rank(
    query=&quot;https://example.com/product.jpg&quot;,
    documents=[&quot;A red sneaker&quot;, &quot;A blue dress&quot;, &quot;A leather bag&quot;],
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">CrossEncoder</span>

<span class="pl-c"># Load a multimodal reranker</span>
<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">CrossEncoder</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Qwen/Qwen3-VL-Reranker-2B"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">revision</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"refs/pr/11"</span>)

<span class="pl-c"># Rank text documents against an image query (or vice versa)</span>
<span class="pl-s1">results</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">rank</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">query</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"https://example.com/product.jpg"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">documents</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[<span class="pl-s">"A red sneaker"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"A blue dress"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"A leather bag"</span>],
)</pre></div>
<p>Two training approaches are provided in the <a href="https://github.com/UKPLab/sentence-transformers/tree/master/examples/cross_encoder/training/multimodal">multimodal training examples</a>:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Any-to-Any + LogitScore</strong>: Uses the full causal LM to generate a single token, scoring via log-odds of "1" vs "0".</li>
<li><strong>Feature Extraction + Pooling + Dense</strong>: More memory-efficient alternative that skips the LM head.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Modular CrossEncoder Architecture (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3549975221" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3554" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3554/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3554">#3554</a>)</h2>
<p><code>CrossEncoder</code> has been fully modularized, inheriting from <code>BaseModel</code> (which is a <code>torch.nn.Sequential</code>). You can now inspect, customize, and compose module chains, just like <code>SentenceTransformer</code>. See the <a href="https://sbert.net/docs/cross_encoder/usage/custom_models.html" rel="nofollow">custom models</a> guide for full details.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from sentence_transformers import CrossEncoder

model = CrossEncoder(&quot;Qwen/Qwen3-Reranker-0.6B&quot;, revision=&quot;refs/pr/11&quot;)
print(model)
&quot;&quot;&quot;
CrossEncoder(
  (0): Transformer({'transformer_task': 'text-generation', ...})
  (1): LogitScore({'true_token_id': 9693, 'false_token_id': 2152, ...})
)
&quot;&quot;&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">CrossEncoder</span>

<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">CrossEncoder</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Qwen/Qwen3-Reranker-0.6B"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">revision</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"refs/pr/11"</span>)
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span>)
<span class="pl-s">"""</span>
<span class="pl-s">CrossEncoder(</span>
<span class="pl-s">  (0): Transformer({'transformer_task': 'text-generation', ...})</span>
<span class="pl-s">  (1): LogitScore({'true_token_id': 9693, 'false_token_id': 2152, ...})</span>
<span class="pl-s">)</span>
<span class="pl-s">"""</span></pre></div>
<h3>Generative reranker support</h3>
<p>Thanks to the modular architecture, generative rerankers like <code>mixedbread-ai/mxbai-rerank-base-v2</code> now work out of the box. These models ship with a <code>modules.json</code> that configures the <code>Transformer</code> + <code>LogitScore</code> chain automatically:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from sentence_transformers import CrossEncoder

model = CrossEncoder(&quot;mixedbread-ai/mxbai-rerank-base-v2&quot;)
scores = model.predict([
    (&quot;How many people live in Berlin?&quot;, &quot;Berlin had a population of 3,520,031 in 2022.&quot;),
    (&quot;How many people live in Berlin?&quot;, &quot;Berlin is well known for its museums.&quot;),
])
# array([ 9. , -0.5], dtype=float32)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">CrossEncoder</span>

<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">CrossEncoder</span>(<span class="pl-s">"mixedbread-ai/mxbai-rerank-base-v2"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">scores</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">predict</span>([
    (<span class="pl-s">"How many people live in Berlin?"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Berlin had a population of 3,520,031 in 2022."</span>),
    (<span class="pl-s">"How many people live in Berlin?"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Berlin is well known for its museums."</span>),
])
<span class="pl-c"># array([ 9. , -0.5], dtype=float32)</span></pre></div>
<h3>Module chain patterns</h3>
<p>The <code>Transformer</code> module now supports multiple task types that determine how the underlying model is loaded and what outputs it produces:</p>
<ul>
<li><code>"sequence-classification"</code>: Loads via <code>AutoModelForSequenceClassification</code>, returns classification logits directly.</li>
<li><code>"text-generation"</code>: Loads via <code>AutoModelForCausalLM</code>, returns raw logits from the language model head.</li>
<li><code>"any-to-any"</code>: Loads via <code>AutoModelForMultimodalLM</code> (transformers v5+), for multimodal causal LMs that accept interleaved image/text inputs.</li>
<li><code>"feature-extraction"</code>: Loads via <code>AutoModel</code> (no task-specific head), returns hidden states.</li>
</ul>
<p>Various module chains are possible now, here's some common ones:</p>
<ol>
<li>
<p>Encoder-based (Sequence Classification): A single <code>Transformer</code> module with <code>transformer_task="sequence-classification"</code>, the traditional BERT/RoBERTa approach. This was previously the only option for CrossEncoder models.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>CausalLM-based (Text Generation + LogitScore): For generative rerankers (Qwen, Llama, mxbai-rerank-v2, etc.), a <code>Transformer</code> with <code>transformer_task="text-generation"</code> followed by a <code>LogitScore</code> module that computes <code>logit["yes"] - logit["no"]</code> at the last token position. For multimodal rerankers, <code>transformer_task="any-to-any"</code> is used instead.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Feature Extraction + Pooling + Dense: A memory-efficient alternative that uses the base model without LM head, pools the last token, and projects to a single score via a Dense layer.</p>
</li>
</ol>
<p>When loading a model without a <code>modules.json</code>, CrossEncoder automatically selects the right chain: if the architecture ends with <code>ForCausalLM</code>, it uses <code>text-generation</code> + <code>LogitScore</code> (with "yes"/"no" tokens); otherwise it uses <code>sequence-classification</code>. You can also construct custom module chains explicitly:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from sentence_transformers import CrossEncoder
from sentence_transformers.cross_encoder.modules import Transformer, LogitScore

transformer = Transformer(&quot;Qwen/Qwen3-Reranker-0.6B&quot;, transformer_task=&quot;text-generation&quot;, revision=&quot;refs/pr/11&quot;)
true_id = transformer.tokenizer.convert_tokens_to_ids(&quot;1&quot;)..."><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">CrossEncoder</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span>.<span class="pl-s1">cross_encoder</span>.<span class="pl-s1">modules</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Transformer</span>, <span class="pl-v">LogitScore</span>

<span class="pl-s1">transformer</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Transformer</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Qwen/Qwen3-Reranker-0.6B"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">transformer_task</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"text-generation"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">revision</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"refs/pr/11"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">true_id</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">transformer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">tokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">convert_tokens_to_ids</span>(<span class="pl-s">"1"</span>)...</pre></div></div>
          <a href="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/releases/tag/v5.4.0" data-view-component="true" class="Link">Read more</a>
</div>

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><p>This minor version brings several improvements to contrastive learning: <code>MultipleNegativesRankingLoss</code> now supports alternative InfoNCE formulations (symmetric, GTE-style) and optional hardness weighting for harder negatives. Two new losses are introduced, <code>GlobalOrthogonalRegularizationLoss</code> for embedding space regularization and <code>CachedSpladeLoss</code> for memory-efficient SPLADE training. The release also adds a faster hashed batch sampler, fixes <code>GroupByLabelBatchSampler</code> for triplet losses, and ensures full compatibility with the latest Transformers v5 versions.</p>
<p>Install this version with</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Training + Inference
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.3.0

# Inference only, use one of:
pip install sentence-transformers==5.3.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.3.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.3.0
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.3.0"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Training + Inference</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.3.0

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Inference only, use one of:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers==5.3.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.3.0
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.3.0
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.3.0</pre></div>
<h2>Updated MultipleNegativesRankingLoss (a.k.a. InfoNCE)</h2>
<p><code>MultipleNegativesRankingLoss</code> received two major upgrades: support for alternative InfoNCE formulations from the literature, and optional hardness weighting to up-weight harder negatives.</p>
<h3>Support other InfoNCE variants (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3783197914" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3607" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3607/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3607">#3607</a>)</h3>
<p><code>MultipleNegativesRankingLoss</code> now supports several well-known contrastive loss variants from the literature through new <code>directions</code> and <code>partition_mode</code> parameters. Previously, this loss only supported the standard forward direction (query → doc). You can now configure which similarity interactions are included in the loss:</p>
<ul>
<li><code>"query_to_doc"</code> (default): For each query, its matched document should score higher than all other documents.</li>
<li><code>"doc_to_query"</code>: The symmetric reverse — for each document, its matched query should score higher than all other queries.</li>
<li><code>"query_to_query"</code>: For each query, all other queries should score lower than its matched document.</li>
<li><code>"doc_to_doc"</code>: For each document, all other documents should score lower than its matched query.</li>
</ul>
<p>The <code>partition_mode</code> controls how scores are normalized: <code>"joint"</code> computes a single softmax over all directions, while <code>"per_direction"</code> computes a separate softmax per direction and averages the losses.</p>
<p>These combine to reproduce several loss formulations from the literature:</p>
<p><strong>Standard InfoNCE</strong> (default, unchanged behavior):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="loss = MultipleNegativesRankingLoss(model)
# equivalent to directions=(&quot;query_to_doc&quot;,), partition_mode=&quot;joint&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-s1">loss</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">MultipleNegativesRankingLoss</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span>)
<span class="pl-c"># equivalent to directions=("query_to_doc",), partition_mode="joint"</span></pre></div>
<p><strong>Symmetric InfoNCE</strong> (<a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.19923" rel="nofollow">Günther et al. 2024</a>) — adds the reverse direction so both queries and documents are trained to find their match:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="loss = MultipleNegativesRankingLoss(
    model,
    directions=(&quot;query_to_doc&quot;, &quot;doc_to_query&quot;),
    partition_mode=&quot;per_direction&quot;,
)"><pre><span class="pl-s1">loss</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">MultipleNegativesRankingLoss</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">directions</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>(<span class="pl-s">"query_to_doc"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"doc_to_query"</span>),
    <span class="pl-s1">partition_mode</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"per_direction"</span>,
)</pre></div>
<p><strong>GTE improved contrastive loss</strong> (<a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.03281" rel="nofollow">Li et al. 2023</a>) — adds same-type negatives (query &lt;-&gt; query, doc &lt;-&gt; doc) for a stronger training signal, especially useful with pairs-only data:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="loss = MultipleNegativesRankingLoss(
    model,
    directions=(&quot;query_to_doc&quot;, &quot;query_to_query&quot;, &quot;doc_to_query&quot;, &quot;doc_to_doc&quot;),
    partition_mode=&quot;joint&quot;,
)"><pre><span class="pl-s1">loss</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">MultipleNegativesRankingLoss</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">directions</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>(<span class="pl-s">"query_to_doc"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"query_to_query"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"doc_to_query"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"doc_to_doc"</span>),
    <span class="pl-s1">partition_mode</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"joint"</span>,
)</pre></div>
<h3>Hardness-weighted contrastive learning (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3964249368" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3667" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3667/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3667">#3667</a>)</h3>
<p>Adds optional hardness weighting to <code>MultipleNegativesRankingLoss</code> and <code>CachedMultipleNegativesRankingLoss</code>, inspired by <a href="https://huggingface.co/papers/2503.04812" rel="nofollow">Lan et al. 2025</a> (LLaVE). This up-weights harder negatives in the softmax by adding <code>hardness_strength * stop_grad(cos_sim)</code> to selected negative logits. The feature is off by default (<code>hardness_mode=None</code>), so existing behavior is unchanged.</p>
<p>The <code>hardness_mode</code> parameter controls which negatives receive the penalty:</p>
<ul>
<li><code>"in_batch_negatives"</code>: Penalizes in-batch negatives only (positives and hard negatives from other samples). Works with all data formats including pairs-only.</li>
<li><code>"hard_negatives"</code>: Penalizes explicit hard negatives only (columns beyond the first two). Only active when hard negatives are provided.</li>
<li><code>"all_negatives"</code>: Penalizes both in-batch and hard negatives, leaving only the positive unpenalized.</li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from sentence_transformers.losses import MultipleNegativesRankingLoss

loss = MultipleNegativesRankingLoss(
    model,
    hardness_mode=&quot;in_batch_negatives&quot;,
    hardness_strength=9.0,
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span>.<span class="pl-s1">losses</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">MultipleNegativesRankingLoss</span>

<span class="pl-s1">loss</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">MultipleNegativesRankingLoss</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">hardness_mode</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"in_batch_negatives"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">hardness_strength</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">9.0</span>,
)</pre></div>
<h2>New loss: GlobalOrthogonalRegularizationLoss (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3891801223" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3654" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3654/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3654">#3654</a>)</h2>
<p>Introduces <code>GlobalOrthogonalRegularizationLoss</code> (<a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/1708.06320" rel="nofollow">Zhang et al. 2017</a>), a regularization loss that encourages embeddings to be well-distributed in the embedding space. It penalizes two things: (1) high mean pairwise similarity across unrelated embeddings, and (2) high second moment of similarities (which indicates clustering). This loss is meant to be combined with a primary contrastive loss like <code>MultipleNegativesRankingLoss</code>. By wrapping both losses in a single module, you can share embeddings and only require one forward pass:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import torch
from datasets import Dataset
from torch import Tensor
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer, SentenceTransformerTrainer
from sentence_transformers.losses import GlobalOrthogonalRegularizationLoss, MultipleNegativesRankingLoss
from sentence_transformers.util import cos_sim

model = SentenceTransformer(&quot;microsoft/mpnet-base&quot;)
train_dataset = Dataset.from_dict({
    &quot;anchor&quot;: [&quot;It's nice weather outside today.&quot;, &quot;He drove to work.&quot;],
    &quot;positive&quot;: [&quot;It's so sunny.&quot;, &quot;He took the car to the office.&quot;],
})

class InfoNCEGORLoss(torch.nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, model: SentenceTransformer, similarity_fct=cos_sim, scale=20.0) -&gt; None:
        super().__init__()
        self.model = model
        self.info_nce_loss = MultipleNegativesRankingLoss(model, similarity_fct=similarity_fct, scale=scale)
        self.gor_loss = GlobalOrthogonalRegularizationLoss(model, similarity_fct=similarity_fct)

    def forward(self, sentence_features: list[dict[str, Tensor]], labels: Tensor | None = None) -&gt; Tensor:
        embeddings = [self.model(sentence_feature)[&quot;sentence_embedding&quot;] for sentence_feature in sentence_features]
        info_nce_loss: dict[str, Tensor] = {
            &quot;info_nce&quot;: self.info_nce_loss.compute_loss_from_embeddings(embeddings, labels)
        }
        gor_loss: dict[str, Tensor] = self.gor_loss.compute_loss_from_embeddings(embeddings, labels)
        return {**info_nce_loss, **gor_loss}

loss = InfoNCEGORLoss(model)
trainer = SentenceTransformerTrainer(
    model=model,
    train_dataset=train_dataset,
    loss=loss,
)
trainer.train()"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">torch</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">datasets</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Dataset</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">torch</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Tensor</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">SentenceTransformer</span>, <span class="pl-v">SentenceTransformerTrainer</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span>.<span class="pl-s1">losses</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">GlobalOrthogonalRegularizationLoss</span>, <span class="pl-v">MultipleNegativesRankingLoss</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span>.<span class="pl-s1">util</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">cos_sim</span>

<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformer</span>(<span class="pl-s">"microsoft/mpnet-base"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">Dataset</span>.<span class="pl-c1">from_dict</span>({
    <span class="pl-s">"anchor"</span>: [<span class="pl-s">"It's nice weather outside today."</span>, <span class="pl-s">"He drove to work."</span>],
    <span class="pl-s">"positive"</span>: [<span class="pl-s">"It's so sunny."</span>, <span class="pl-s">"He took the car to the office."</span>],
})

<span class="pl-k">class</span> <span class="pl-v">InfoNCEGORLoss</span>(<span class="pl-s1">torch</span>.<span class="pl-c1">nn</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Module</span>):
    <span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">__init__</span>(<span class="pl-s1">self</span>, <span class="pl-s1">model</span>: <span class="pl-smi">SentenceTransformer</span>, <span class="pl-s1">similarity_fct</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">cos_sim</span>, <span class="pl-s1">scale</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">20.0</span>) <span class="pl-c1">-&gt;</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span>:
        <span class="pl-en">super</span>().<span class="pl-c1">__init__</span>()
        <span class="pl-s1">self</span>.<span class="pl-c1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>
        <span class="pl-s1">self</span>.<span class="pl-c1">info_nce_loss</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">MultipleNegativesRankingLoss</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span>, <span class="pl-s1">similarity_fct</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">similarity_fct</span>, <span class="pl-s1">scale</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">scale</span>)
        <span class="pl-s1">self</span>.<span class="pl-c1">gor_loss</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">GlobalOrthogonalRegularizationLoss</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span>, <span class="pl-s1">similarity_fct</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">similarity_fct</span>)

    <span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">forward</span>(<span class="pl-s1">self</span>, <span class="pl-s1">sentence_features</span>: <span class="pl-s1">list</span>[<span class="pl-s1">dict</span>[<span class="pl-smi">str</span>, <span class="pl-smi">Tensor</span>]], <span class="pl-s1">labels</span>: <span class="pl-v">Tensor</span> <span class="pl-c1">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span>) <span class="pl-c1">-&gt;</span> <span class="pl-smi">Tensor</span>:
        <span class="pl-s1">embeddings</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> [<span class="pl-s1">self</span>.<span class="pl-c1">model</span>(<span class="pl-s1">sentence_feature</span>)[<span class="pl-s">"sentence_embedding"</span>] <span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_feature</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_features</span>]
        <span class="pl-s1">info_nce_loss</span>: <span class="pl-s1">dict</span>[<span class="pl-smi">str</span>, <span class="pl-smi">Tensor</span>] <span class="pl-c1">=</span> {
            <span class="pl-s">"info_nce"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">self</span>.<span class="pl-c1">info_nce_loss</span>.<span class="pl-c1">compute_loss_from_embeddings</span>(<span class="pl-s1">embeddings</span>, <span class="pl-s1">labels</span>)
        }
        <span class="pl-s1">gor_loss</span>: <span class="pl-s1">dict</span>[<span class="pl-smi">str</span>, <span class="pl-smi">Tensor</span>] <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">self</span>.<span class="pl-c1">gor_loss</span>.<span class="pl-c1">compute_loss_from_embeddings</span>(<span class="pl-s1">embeddings</span>, <span class="pl-s1">labels</span>)
        <span class="pl-k">return</span> {<span class="pl-c1">**</span><span class="pl-s1">info_nce_loss</span>, <span class="pl-c1">**</span><span class="pl-s1">gor_loss</span>}

<span class="pl-s1">loss</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">InfoNCEGORLoss</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">trainer</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformerTrainer</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">model</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">loss</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">loss</span>,
)
<span class="pl-s1">trainer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">train</span>()</pre></div>
<h2>New loss: CachedSpladeLoss for memory-efficient SPLADE training (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3968746816" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3670" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3670/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3670">#3670</a>)</h2>
<p>Introduces <code>CachedSpladeLoss</code>, a gradient-cached version of <code>SpladeLoss</code> that enables training SPLADE models with larger batch sizes without additional GPU memory. It applies the GradCache technique at the <code>SpladeLoss</code> wrapper level, so both the base loss and regularizers receive pre-computed embeddings — no changes to existing base losses or regularizers are needed.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from datasets import Dataset
from sentence_transformers.sparse_encoder import SparseEncoder, SparseEncoderTrainer
from sentence_transformers.sparse_encoder.losses import CachedSpladeLoss, SparseMultipleNegativesRankingLoss

model = SparseEncoder(&quot;distilbert/distilbert-base-uncased&quot;)
train_dataset = Dataset.from_dict({
    &quot;anchor&quot;: [&quot;It's nice weather outside today.&quot;, &quot;He drove to work.&quot;],
    &quot;positive&quot;: [&quot;It's so sunny.&quot;, &quot;He took the car to the office.&quot;],
})
loss = CachedSpladeLoss(
    model=model,
    loss=SparseMultipleNegativesRankingLoss(model),
    document_regularizer_weight=3e-5,
    query_regularizer_weight=5e-5,
    mini_batch_size=32,
)

trainer = SparseEncoderTrainer(model=model, train_dataset=train_dataset, loss=loss)
trainer.train()"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">datasets</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Dataset</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span>.<span class="pl-s1">sparse_encoder</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">SparseEncoder</span>, <span class="pl-v">SparseEncoderTrainer</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span>.<span class="pl-s1">sparse_encoder</span>.<span class="pl-s1">losses</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">CachedSpladeLoss</span>, <span class="pl-v">SparseMultipleNegativesRankingLoss</span>

<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SparseEncoder</span>(<span class="pl-s">"distilbert/distilbert-base-uncased"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">Dataset</span>.<span class="pl-c1">from_dict</span>({
    <span class="pl-s">"anchor"</span>: [<span class="pl-s">"It's nice weather outside today."</span>, <span class="pl-s">"He drove to work."</span>],
    <span class="pl-s">"positive"</span>: [<span class="pl-s">"It's so sunny."</span>, <span class="pl-s">"He took the car to the office."</span>],
})
<span class="pl-s1">loss</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">CachedSpladeLoss</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">model</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">loss</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">SparseMultipleNegativesRankingLoss</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span>),
    <span class="pl-s1">document_regularizer_weight</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">3e-5</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">query_regularizer_weight</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">5e-5</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">mini_batch_size</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">32</span>,
)

<span class="pl-s1">trainer</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SparseEncoderTrainer</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">model</span>, <span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">train_dataset</span>, <span class="pl-s1">loss</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">loss</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">trainer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">train</span>()</pre></div>
<h2>Faster NoDuplicatesBatchSampler with hashing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3795839835" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3611" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3611/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3611">#3611</a>)</h2>
<p>Adds a <code>NO_DUPLICATES_HASHED</code> batch sampler option, which uses the existing <code>NoDuplicatesBatchSampler</code> with <code>precompute_hashes=True</code>. This pre-computes xxhash 64-bit values for each sample, providing significant speedups for large batch sizes at a small memory cost. Requires the <code>xxhash</code> library.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformerTrainingArguments

args = SentenceTransformerTrainingArguments(
    batch_sampler=&quot;NO_DUPLICATES_HASHED&quot;  # Pre-computes hashes for faster duplicate checking
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">sentence_transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">SentenceTransformerTrainingArguments</span>

<span class="pl-s1">args</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SentenceTransformerTrainingArguments</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">batch_sampler</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"NO_DUPLICATES_HASHED"</span>  <span class="pl-c"># Pre-computes hashes for faster duplicate checking</span>
)</pre></div>
<h2>GroupByLabelBatchSampler improvements for triplet losses (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3965602563" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3668" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3668/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3668">#3668</a>)</h2>
<p>Fixes a critical issue where <code>GroupByLabelBatchSampler</code> produced ~99% single-class batches, causing zero gradients with triplet losses. The sampler now uses round-robin interleaving where each label emits 2 samples per round, with the label visit order reshuffled every round. This guarantees every batch contains multiple distinct labels, each with at least 2 samples.</p>
<h2>Transformers v5 compatibility</h2>
<p>This release includes full compatibility updates for Transformers v5:</p>
<ul>
<li>Compatibility with transformers 5.0.0rc01 and later versions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3729981656" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3597" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3597/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3597">#3597</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3855478840" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3615" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3615/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3615">#3615</a>)</li>
<li>Support for T5Gemma and T5Gemma2 models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3886192719" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3644" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3644/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3644">#3644</a>)</li>
<li>Transformers v5.2 compatibility for the trainer's <code>_nested_gather</code> method (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3952376109" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3664" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3664/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3664">#3664</a>)</li>
<li>Support for both <code>warmup_steps</code> and <code>warmup_ratio</code> until Transformers v4 support is dropped (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3886556995" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3645" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3645/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3645">#3645</a>)</li>
<li>Updated CI to test against full Transformers v5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3855478840" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3615" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3615/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3615">#3615</a>)</li>
</ul>

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><p>This patch release introduces compatibility with Transformers v5.2.</p>
<p>Install this version with</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Training + Inference
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.2.3

# Inference only, use one of:
pip install sentence-transformers==5.2.3
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.2.3
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.2.3
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.2.3"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Training + Inference</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.2.3

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Inference only, use one of:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers==5.2.3
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.2.3
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.2.3
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.2.3</pre></div>
<h2>Transformers v5.2 Support</h2>
<p><a href="https://github.com/huggingface/transformers/releases/tag/v5.2.0">Transformers v5.2</a> has just released, and it updated its <code>Trainer</code> in such a way that training with Sentence Transformers would start failing on the logging step. The <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3952376109" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3664" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3664/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3664">#3664</a> pull request has resolved this issue.</p>
<p>If you're not training with Sentence Transformers, then older versions of Sentence Transformers are also compatible with Transformers v5.2.</p>
<h2>All Changes</h2>
<ul>
<li>[<code>compat</code>] Introduce Transformers v5.2 compatibility: trainer _nested_gather moved by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/tomaarsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/tomaarsen">@tomaarsen</a> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3952376109" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3664" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3664/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3664">#3664</a>)</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Full Changelog</strong>: <a class="commit-link" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/compare/v5.2.2...v5.2.3"><tt>v5.2.2...v5.2.3</tt></a></p></div>
</div>

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><p>This patch release replaces mandatory <code>requests</code> dependency with an optional <code>httpx</code> dependency.</p>
<p>Install this version with</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Training + Inference
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.2.2

# Inference only, use one of:
pip install sentence-transformers==5.2.2
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.2.2
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.2.2
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.2.2"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Training + Inference</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.2.2

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Inference only, use one of:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers==5.2.2
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.2.2
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.2.2
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.2.2</pre></div>
<h2>Transformers v5 Support</h2>
<p><a href="https://github.com/huggingface/transformers/releases/tag/v5.0.0">Transformers v5.0</a> and its required <code>huggingface_hub</code> versions have dropped support of <code>requests</code> in favor of <code>httpx</code>. The former was also used in <code>sentence-transformers</code>, but not listed explicitly as a dependency. This patch removes the use of <code>requests</code> in favor of <code>httpx</code>, although it's now optional and not automatically imported. This should also save some import time.</p>
<p>Importing Sentence Transformers should now not crash if <code>requests</code> is not installed.</p>
<h2>All Changes</h2>
<ul>

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><p>This patch release adds support for the full <a href="https://github.com/huggingface/transformers/releases/tag/v5.0.0">Transformers v5 release</a>.</p>
<p>Install this version with</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Training + Inference
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.2.1

# Inference only, use one of:
pip install sentence-transformers==5.2.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.2.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.2.1
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.2.1"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Training + Inference</span>
pip install sentence-transformers[train]==5.2.1

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Inference only, use one of:</span>
pip install sentence-transformers==5.2.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx-gpu]==5.2.1
pip install sentence-transformers[onnx]==5.2.1
pip install sentence-transformers[openvino]==5.2.1</pre></div>
<h2>Transformers v5 Support</h2>
<p>Sentence Transformers v5.2.0 already introduced support for the Transformers v5.0 release candidates, but this release is adding support for the full release. The intention is to maintain backward compatibility with v4.x. The library includes dual CI testing for both version for now, allowing users to upgrade to the newest Transformers features when ready. In future versions, Sentence Transformers may start requiring Transformers v5.0 or higher.</p>
<h2>All Changes</h2>
<ul>
<li>Introduce compatibility with transformers 5.0.0rc01 by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/tomaarsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/tomaarsen">@tomaarsen</a> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3729981656" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3597" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3597/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3597">#3597</a>)</li>
<li>Specify numpy manually in dependencies, as it's directly used/imported by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/tomaarsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/tomaarsen">@tomaarsen</a> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3785243044" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3608" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3608/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3608">#3608</a>)</li>
<li>Expand test suite to full transformers v5 by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/tomaarsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/tomaarsen">@tomaarsen</a> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3855478840" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/issues/3615" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3615/hovercard" href="https://github.com/huggingface/sentence-transformers/pull/3615">#3615</a>)</li>
